<style id='tainacan-embeds-inline-inline-css'>
/* TAINACAN EMBEDS 
		* This file copies most of Gutenberg's logic for responsive blocks,
		* but uses different classes to avoid future conflicts.
		* Check their original css: /packages/block-library/src/embed/style.scss
		*/

		/* 
		* The embed container is in a `figure` element, and many themes zero this out.
		* This rule explicitly sets it, to ensure at least some bottom-margin in the flow.
		*/
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-content-embed {
			margin-bottom: 1em;
			margin-left: 0;
			margin-right: 0;
			clear: both;
		}
		/* Don't allow iframe to overflow it's container. */
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-content-embed iframe {
			max-width: 100%;
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-content-embed .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper {
			position: relative;
		}
		/* Add responsiveness to embeds with aspect ratios. */
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-has-aspect-ratio .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			content: "";
			display: block;
			padding-top: 50%; /* Default to 2:1 aspect ratio. */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-has-aspect-ratio iframe {
			position: absolute;
			top: 0;
			right: 0;
			bottom: 0;
			left: 0;
			height: 100%;
			width: 100%;
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-21-9 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 42.85%; /* 9 / 21 * 100 */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-18-9 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 50%; /* 9 / 18 * 100 */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-16-9 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 56.25%; /* 9 / 16 * 100 */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-4-3 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 75%; /* 3 / 4 * 100 */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-1-1 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 100%; /* 1 / 1 * 100 */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-9-16 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 177.77%; /* 16 / 9 * 100 */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-3-4 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 133.33%; /* 4 / 3 * 100 */
		}
		:not(.wp-block-embed__wrapper)>.tainacan-embed-aspect-1-2 .tainacan-content-embed__wrapper::before {
			padding-top: 200%; /* 2 / 1 * 100 */
		}

<h1 class="wp-block-post-title has-text-color has-white-color">Jurisprudência</h1></div>
</div>
</header>


<div class="wp-block-group is-layout-flow wp-block-group-is-layout-flow" style="padding-right:2rem;padding-left:2rem">
<div class="wp-block-group is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained" style="padding-top:6rem;padding-bottom:6rem"><div class="entry-content has-text-color wp-block-post-content is-layout-flow wp-block-post-content-is-layout-flow">
<p>Nesta página é possível o acesso às ferramentas de jurisprudência já desenvolvidas pelo Tribunal de Contas dos Municípios do Estado de Goiás.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity"/>



<span id="more-15293"></span>



<p><br></p>


<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-4"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><a href="https://virtual.tcmgo.tc.br/jurisprudencia/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img decoding="async" class="alignright" src="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/Screenshot_1.png" alt="Jurisprudência" width="200" height="100" /></a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-3-5"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h5><a href="https://virtual.tcmgo.tc.br/jurisprudencia/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="color: #cea800;"><strong>Jurisprudência</strong></span></a></h5>
<p>Ferramentas de jurisprudência desenvolvidas pelo TCM GO</p>
</div></div>
</div>


<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-4"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/informativo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img decoding="async" class="alignright" src="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/btn_informativodejurisprudencia_lateral.png" alt="Informativo de Jurisprudência" width="200" height="100" /></a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-3-5"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h5><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/informativo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="color: #cea800;"><strong>Informativo de Jurisprudência</strong></span></a></h5>
<p>Boletim mensal contendo o resumo de Acórdãos selecionados que contenham matéria de relevância jurídica, contábil ou econômica.</p>
</div></div>
</div>


<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-4"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/jurisprudencia-selecionada-de-controle-externo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img decoding="async" class="alignright" src="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/btn_jurisprudencia_selecionada_de_controle_externo.png" alt="Jurisprudência Selecionada de Controle Externo" width="200" height="100" /></a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-3-5"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h5><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/jurisprudencia-selecionada-de-controle-externo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="color: #cea800;"><strong>Jurisprudência Selecionada de Controle Externo</strong></span></a></h5>
<p>Destaca as decisões selecionadas dos tribunais superiores e dos principais tribunais de contas estaduais, nas diversas áreas de atuação do controle externo.</p>
</div></div>
</div>


<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-4"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/leisanotadas/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="alignright" src="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/btn_leisanotadastcmgo.jpg" alt="Leis Anotadas TCMGO" width="200" height="100" /></a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-3-5"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h5><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/leisanotadas/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="color: #cea800;"><strong>Leis Anotadas TCMGO</strong></span></a></h5>
<p>Legislação de relevância para a administração Pública e o Controle Externo anotada com Consultas e Acórdãos selecionados do TCMGO.</p>
</div></div>
</div>


<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-4"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/legislacao/accon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="alignright" src="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/btn_acordaodeconsultas_horizontal.png" alt="Acórdãos de Consulta" width="200" height="100" /></a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-3-5"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h5><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/legislacao/accon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="color: #cea800;"><strong>Acórdãos de Consultas</strong></span></a></h5>
<p>Contendo o ementário das decisões proferidas pelo TCMGO com caráter normativo que constituem prejulgamento de tese, nos termos do art. 199, §3º, do Regimento Interno.</p>
</div></div>
</div>


<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-4"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/sumula/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="alignright" src="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/wp-content/uploads/2019/06/jurisprudencia_banner_enunciadosdesumula.png" alt="Enunciados de Súmula" width="200" height="100" /></a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-3-5"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h5><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/sumula/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="color: #cea800;"><strong>Enunciados de Súmula</strong></span></a></h5>
<p>Contendo os Enunciados de Súmula aprovados pelo TCMGO, nos termos da RA nº 060/19.</p>
</div></div>
</div>


<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-4"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/boas-praticas/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="alignright" src="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/btn_boaspraticasdejurisprudencia.png" alt="Boas práticas de Jurisprudência" width="200" height="100" /></a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-3-5"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h5><a href="https://www.tcmgo.tc.br/site/jurisprudencia/boas-praticas/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="color: #cea800;"><strong>Boas práticas de Jurisprudência</strong></span></a></h5>
<p>Seleção de instrumentos e sistemas de pesquisa de jurisprudência, disponibilizados por outros Tribunais.</p>
</div></div>
</div>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity"/>



<div style="text-align: center; padding-bottom: 60px; font-size: 15px;"><strong>Contatos, dúvidas e sugestões:</strong><br>Superintendência de Secretaria<br>jurisprudencia@tcm.go.gov.br<br>(62) 3216.6147</div>
</div></div>
</div>

<script id="wp-block-template-skip-link-js-after">
	( function() {
		var skipLinkTarget = document.querySelector( 'main' ),
			sibling,
			skipLinkTargetID,
			skipLink;

		// Early exit if a skip-link target can't be located.
		if ( ! skipLinkTarget ) {
			return;
		}

		/*
		 * Get the site wrapper.
		 * The skip-link will be injected in the beginning of it.
		 */
		sibling = document.querySelector( '.wp-site-blocks' );

		// Early exit if the root element was not found.
		if ( ! sibling ) {
			return;
		}

		// Get the skip-link target's ID, and generate one if it doesn't exist.
		skipLinkTargetID = skipLinkTarget.id;
		if ( ! skipLinkTargetID ) {
			skipLinkTargetID = 'wp--skip-link--target';
			skipLinkTarget.id = skipLinkTargetID;
		}

		// Create the skip link.
		skipLink = document.createElement( 'a' );
		skipLink.classList.add( 'skip-link', 'screen-reader-text' );
		skipLink.id = 'wp-skip-link';
		skipLink.href = '#' + skipLinkTargetID;
		skipLink.innerText = 'Pular para o conteúdo';

		// Inject the skip link.
		sibling.parentElement.insertBefore( skipLink, sibling );
	}() );
